Exact internal score:
```
def conv_full(a):
    n=len(a); M=2*n-1; L=1<<(M-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:M]
```
For n=10000, L=32768? M=19999, L=32768. fast.

Score:
```
def score_of(a):
   c=conv_full(a); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-9: inf; return 2*n*max(c)/S*S
```
Normalize:
```
def normalize(a): s=a.sum(); if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n; else a=a/s; return a
```

Golden search details:
Given P arrays p_lags (int64 length P), cP = c[p_lags] copy? We update c later. For current x0, S. Define function gP(x):
  delta = x - x0
  val = cP.copy()
  mask = (p >= j) & (p < j+n)
  if any:
    seg_idx = p[mask] - j
    # For p == 2j, quadratic replace
    quad_mask = mask & (p == 2*j)
    lin_mask = mask & (p != 2*j)
    val[lin_mask] += 2*delta * a[pg[lin_mask]]  # p-j index
    val[quad_mask] -= x0*x0
    val[quad_mask] += x*x
  # p outside unchanged
  S_new = S + delta

But exact candidate evaluation with rollback apply_delta each candidate. Need implement efficient `try_delta` returning score and applying/rollback. We can write helper:
```
def eval_candidate(a, c, n, j, S, x):
    x0 = a[j]
    delta = x - x0
    if abs(delta) < 1e-15*max(1,abs(x0)): return S, 1e100
    apply_delta(a,c,n,j,delta)
    S_new = S+delta
    maxc = c.max()
    sc = TWO_N*maxc/(S_new*S_new)
    # rollback
    apply_delta(a,c,n,j,-delta) # now a[j]=x, -delta returns x0
    S_new = S
    return x, sc
```
But apply_delta uses `c[j:j+n] += 2.0*delta*a`. For rollback, a[j] currently x, and -delta, okay. However after candidate, if we hold a[j]=x and c updated? Then apply_delta(-delta) uses a with x. Good. It updates c to original and a to x0. But note for quad correction, apply_delta adds delta^2 always. For rollback delta=-delta, adds delta^2, as reasoned. Good.

Now KKT update helper.
```
def kkt_update(a, c, S, p_lags, weights, mix):
    n=len(a)
    b=np.zeros(n)
    # if weights uniform top? ensure sum weights maybe not 1; b scaled doesn't matter for normalize.
    for w,k in zip(weights,p_lags):
        if w==0: continue
        js = max(0, k-(n-1)); je=min(n-1,k)
        # i = k-j; when j=js, i=k-js; when j=je, i=k-je.
        i_start = k - je; i_end = k - js
        # a slice i_start:i_end+1 reversed length je-js+1
        b[js:je+1] += w * a[i_start:i_end+1][::-1]
    s = b.sum()
    if s <= 0: return None, inf
    b = b/s
    a_new = (1-mix)*a + mix*b
    a_new = clip nonneg, if sum<... normalize to S? For score independent, we can normalize to sum 1. But if a sum original 1, mix with normalized b preserves sum 1 exactly if b sum1 and a sum1. Good. S_new=1.
    c_new=conv_full(a_new)
    sc=...
    accept if sc<best.
```

Coordinate descent function:
```
def coordinate_descent(a, c, S, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, P_size=64, candidates_per_coord=3, verbose=True):
    # a sum ~1, c exact, S~1
    best_score = score_from_a_c(a,c)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_S = S
    TWO_N = 2.0*n
    tmp = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    no_improve=0
    pass_count=0
    # maybe preallocate lags
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 3 and pass_count < max_passes:
       # top P
       p_idx = suptop(c, P_size)
       # order random permutation
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       improved=False
       # maybe periodically recompute top P and c? within pass every N
       for count, j in enumerate(order):
          if time...
          x0 = a[j]
          # generate candidates
          cands = generate_candidates(...)
          # evaluate exact for each
          best_x=x0; best_sc=best_score? Actually current score after previous accepted coordinate. We can track current_score = TWO_N*c.max()/S*S. After accepting coordinate, update.
          for x in cands:
             delta=x-x0
             if abs(delta) < 1e-14*max(1,abs(x0)): continue
             apply_delta(...)
             S_new=S+delta
             maxc=c.max()
             sc = TWO_N*maxc/(S_new*S_new)
             if sc < best_sc:
                 best_sc=sc; best_x=x; best_delta=delta
             # rollback
             apply_delta(..., -delta, tmp)
          if best_x != x0:
             apply_delta(best_delta)
             S += best_delta
             current_score=best_sc
             if current_score < best_score - 1e-12: best_score=current_score; best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_S=S
             improved=True
             # if after many updates S drift large? normalize? maybe if abs(S-1)>0.1: normalize and recompute c? But mid pass? We can normalize less often.
       # end pass
       # recompute c exactly (via FFT) to remove numerical drift
       a = normalize_sum(a, 1.0) maybe if S drift; S=1
       c = conv_full(a)
       sc = score_from_a_c(a,c)
       if sc < best_score: best...
       if sc > best_score + small: restore best? Since greedy accepted exact should not degrade, but KKT or normalization maybe. We can restore best_a/c if current worse.
       if sc < best_pass_start: no_improve=0 else no_improve++
       pass_count++
    return best_score, best_a, best_c, best_S
```
But careful: `best_score` tracking across coordinates: Since we accept only if candidate score < current_score (not previous best). If no coordinate improves current, current remains. def surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, x, x0, S):
    delta=x-x0
    Sn=S+delta
    if Sn <= 0: return 1e100
    denom=Sn*Sn
    val = base_out_max
    if base_lin.size:
       # max(base_lin + 2*delta*coeff)

    if base_quad_val > -1e300:
       q=base_quad_val + x*x
       if q>val: val=q
    return val/denom

KKT update helper:
```
def kkt_proposal(a, c, p_idx, weights, mix, n):
    b = np.zeros(n)
    for idx in range(len(p_idx)):
       w=weights[idx]; k=int(p_idx[idx]); if w==0 continue
       js = max(0, k - (n-1)); je=min(n-1, k)
       i_start = k - je; i_end = k - js
       # need reversed a slice
       b[js:je+1] += w * a[i_start:i_end+1][::-1]
    s=b.sum()
    if s <=0: return None
    b /= s
    return (1-mix)*a + mix*b
```
Top-level. It allocates b. We can call for a few top sets. Accept if exact score lower.
But if we do KKT after coordinate pass, and it improves, we need update c.

- Maybe `w = exp((ct - ct.max()) / temp)`; but ct small. Use ratio to mean: temp=max(ct.std(), 1e-12). w=np.exp((ct-ct.max())/temp); normalize.
Try all for m in [32,64,128] and mixes [0.1,0.3,0.6]. But each conv cost. Limit based on time. KKT cheap; can run e.g. every 10 passes a small set (9 proposals) ~9 conv 0.05s. Good. If any improves, accept and maybe repeat.

Top-level `surrogate_value` with arrays:

def surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, x, x0, S):
    Sn = S + (x - x0)
    if Sn <= 1e-300: return 1e100
    denom = Sn*Sn
    val = base_out_max
    if base_lin is not None and base_lin.size:
       # compute maxLin without allocating? Use np.max(base_lin + ...) okay
       m = np.max(base_lin + 2.0*(x-x0)*coeff)
       if m > val: val = m
    if base_quad_val > -1e300:
       q = base_quad_val + x*x
       if q > val: val = q
    return val/denom
```

`surrogate_best_x`: need compute masks. Use lags int array. P small. Implementation:
```
def surrogate_best_x(a, c, S, n, j, x0, lags, hi, evals=14):
    if hi <= x0 + 1e-15:
       hi = max(x0*1.2, x0 + max(S/n,1e-9))
    P=lags.size
    if P==0: return x0
    seg = (lags >= j) & (lags < j+n)
    out = ~seg
    base = c[lags]  # copy
    if out.any(): base_out_max=float(base[out].max())
    else: base_out_max=-np.inf
    lin = seg & (lags != 2*j)
    if lin.any():
       lin_lags=lags[lin]
       base_lin=base[lin]
       coeff=a[lin_lags-j]
    else:
       base_lin=np.empty(0); coeff=np.empty(0)
    quad = seg & (lags == 2*j)
    if quad.any(): base_quad_val=float(c[2*j] - x0*x0)
    else: base_quad_val=-np.inf
    # evaluate at x0 maybe
    lo=0.0; ub=hi
    gr=(np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    c1=ub - gr*(ub-lo); c2=lo + gr*(ub-lo)
    f1=surrogate_value(...); f2=...
    for _ in range(evals):
       if f1 < f2:
          ub=c2; c2=c1; f2=f1; c1=ub - gr*(ub-lo); f1=surrogate_value(...)

def suptop(c, m):
   L=len(c)
   k=min(m,L)
   if k==L: return np.arange(L)
   part = np.argpartition(c, L-k)[L-k:] ? Actually want largest.
   idx = np.argpartition(-c, k-1)[:k] or np.partition(c, L-k)[-k:]
   return idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
```

    best_sc=score...

Detailed implementation of `apply_delta`:
```
def apply_delta(a, c, n, j, delta, tmp):
    if abs(delta) < 1e-300:
        return
    two_delta = 2.0 * delta
    np.multiply(a, two_delta, out=tmp)
    c[j:j+n] += tmp
    c[j + j] += delta * delta
    a[j] += delta
```
But if `a[j]` becomes negative due to candidate fallback? If candidate x=0 and x0 small, apply_delta delta=-x0, a[j] ~0. If due to rounding -1e-20, okay. But then `tmp` for rollback? def top_indices_forced(c, k, forced):
   L=len(c); take=min(k,L); part = np.argpartition(c, L-take)[-take:] if take<L else np.arange(L)
   order = part[np.argsort(c[part])[::-1]]
   # add forced not present
   out_list = list(order[:take])
   for f in forced:
      if 0<=f<L and f not in out_list: out_list.append(f)
   arr=np.array(out_list, dtype=int)
   return arr
```

def kkt_refine(a, c, n, p_idx, deadline, max_seconds=30.0):
    best_sc=...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    if len(p_idx) < 4: return ...
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    m_vals=[min(n? maybe len p, 16), min(len,64), min(len,256)]
    for m in m_vals:
       if m<=0 break
       lags=p_idx[:m]
       ct=c[lags]
       # if all ct same? uniform fine.
       weights_list = []
       weights_list.append(np.full(m, 1.0/m))
       s=ct.sum(); if s>0: weights_list.append(ct/s)
       s2=float((ct*ct).sum()); if s2>0: weights_list.append((ct*ct)/s2)
       # maybe softmax
       ct_centered=ct-ct.max(); temp=max(float(ct.std()), ct.max()*0.1, 1e-15); w=np.exp(ct_centered/temp); s=w.sum(); if s>0: weights_list.append(w/s)
       for w in weights_list:
          b=hamiltonian_sparse(a,lags,w,n)
          bs=b.sum()
          if bs <=0: continue
          b /= bs
          for mix in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.8,1.0]:
             if time.monotonic() > min(deadline, t_start+max_seconds): break...
             cand=(1-mix)*a + mix*b
             cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
             cs=cand.sum()
             if cs <=0: continue
             cand /= cs
             sc=score_of(cand)
             if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                 best_sc=sc; best_a=cand.copy()
    # recompute c for best? We can compute c_best at end to return.
    best_c=conv_full(best_a) if best_sc improved? Could compute if best_a is a? We'll outside recompute.
    return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def random_smooth_initial(n, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    a=np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(rng.integers(3,8)):
       c=rng.uniform(0.05,0.95)
       w=rng.uniform(0.02,0.25)
       amp=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
       a += amp*np.exp(-((x-c)/w)**2)
    a += 0.05
    return normalize_sum(a,1.0)
```
Maybe create with oscillatory: plus sin frequencies. But okay.

Perturbation:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,len(a))
    noise = strength*rng.standard_normal(len(a))
    # smooth noise by convolve small gaussian? Use np.convolve with kernel size 5?
    if strength>0.02:
       kernel=np.ones(5)/5; noise=np.convolve(noise,kernel,mode='same')
    a2=a*np.exp(noise)
    a2 += 1e-3*strength
    return normalize_sum(a2,1.0)
```
But if parent n large, convolve O(n). Fine.
Modulation:
```
def modulate_sequence(a, rng):
    n=len(a); x=np.arange(n)/n
    a2=a.copy()
    for freq in [1,2,3,5]: amp=rng.uniform(0.01,0.05); phase=rng.uniform(0,2π); a2 *= (1+amp*np.cos(2π*freq*x+phase))
    return normalize_sum(clip(a2,0),1)
```

Resample:
```
def resample_sequence(a, m):
   n=len(a)
   if m==n: return a.copy()
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=a
   xm=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   b=np.interp(xm,x,y)
   b=np.clip(b,0,None)
   return normalize_sum(b,1)
```
`np.interp` requires x increasing; okay. Endpoint values interpolated.

Dedup:
```
def dedupe_candidates(cands, x0, lo=0.0, hi=None, tol=1e-10):
   out=[]
   for x in cands:
      if not np.isfinite(x): continue
      if x < 0: x=0.0
      if hi is not None and x > hi: x=hi
      dup=False
      for y in out:
         if abs(x-y) <= tol*max(1.0,abs(y)): dup=True; break
      if not dup: out.append(x)
   return out
```
Top-level.

Need `tol` relative to x values ~1e-4, using max(1,abs) gives absolute 1e-10, okay. Could use 1e-8? If too high skip small moves. Use 1e-12*max(1,abs) =>1e-12. x differences 1e-6 accepted. Good.

Now coordinate_descent detailed pseudocode:
```
def coordinate_descent(a_in, c_in, S_in, n, deadline, rng, p_size=96, max_cand=3, verbose=True):
    a = a_in.astype(np.float64).copy()
    c = c_in.astype(np.float64).copy()
    S = float(S_in)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0: a /= s; S=1.0; c /= s/s? Actually if a_in may not sum=1, we should normalize before. We can outside. But ensure:
       c = conv? Simpler: outside normalize. Here just S=sum(a); if S !=1, scale a,c: if S>0: a/=S; c/=(S*S); S=1.
    current_score = 2n*c.max()
    best_score=current_score; best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    tmp=np.empty(n)
    forced=[0, 2*n-2, n-1]
    no_improve=0; pass_count=0; start_score=current_score
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 4:
       pass_count +=1
       start_score = current_score
       p_idx = top_indices_forced(c, p_size, forced, n)
       top_lags = top_indices(c, min(384, len(c))) maybe for KKT? We'll compute later.
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       max_a = float(a.max())
       avg = S/n
       accepted_count=0
       for count, j in enumerate(order):
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           x0 = float(a[j])
           # if x0 zero and no candidates? still
           max_bound = max( max_a, avg, 1e-12)
           # update p_idx every 512
           if count % 512 == 511:
               p_idx = top_indices_forced(c, p_size, forced, n)
               max_a = float(a.max()); avg = S/n
           hi = ...
           xg = surrogate_best_x(...)
           cands = make...
           best_x=x0; best_sc=current_score
           for x in cands:
              delta = x - x0
              if abs(delta) <= 1e-14*max(1,abs(x0)): continue
              if S + delta <= 0: continue
              apply_delta(a,c,n,j,delta,tmp)
              S_new = S + delta
              maxc = float(c.max())
              sc = 2n*maxc/(S_new*S_new)
              if sc + 1e-13 < best_sc:
                  best_sc=sc; best_x=x; best_delta=delta
              apply_delta(a,c,n,j,-delta,tmp)
           if best_x != x0:
              apply_delta(a,c,n,j,best_delta,tmp)
              S += best_delta
              current_score = best_sc
              accepted_count +=1
              if current_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                  best_score=current_score
                  # periodic copy? We can copy every 256 accepted to avoid losing.
                  if accepted_count % 256 ==0 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 30:
                      best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_S=S
              # if S drift
              if S < 0.5 or S > 1.5:
                  oldS=S; a = a/oldS; c = c/(oldS*oldS); S=1.0; current_score=2n*float(c.max())
                  if current_score < best_score: update
       # end pass
       # normalize and recompute
       oldS=float(a.sum())
       if oldS > 0:
          a = a/oldS; c = c/(oldS*oldS); S=1.0
       else: break
       # recompute exact c to correct drift
       c = conv_full(a)
       current_score = 2n*float(c.max())
       start_pass = start_score
       if current_score < start_pass - 1e-11:
           no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if current_score < best_score - 1e-13:
           best_score=current_score; best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
       # KKT if no_improve or periodic
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
          p_big = top_indices(c, min(512, len(c)))
          # maybe only if no_improve>0 or pass_count%10==0
          if no_improve > 0 or pass_count % 10 == 0:
             sc_k, a_k = kkt_refine(a,c,n,p_big, deadline, max_seconds=60)
             if sc_k + 1e-13 < best_score:
                 best_score=sc_k; best_a=a_k.copy(); best_c=conv_full(a_k); a=best_a; c=best_c; S=1.0; no_improve=0
             elif sc_k + 1e-13 < current_score:
                 current_score=sc_k; a=a_k; c=best_c; S=1.0
       # If best_a improved but current not, maybe set current=best if current worse?
       if current_score > best_score + 1e-8:
           a=best_a.copy(); c=conv_full(a); S=1.0; current_score=best_score
    # final
    final_a = best_a

def run_candidate(a_in, state, deadline, rng, label, max_seconds=None):
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size < 2: return
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <=1e-9: return
    a = a/s
    c = conv_full(a)
    sc = 2*len(a)*float(c.max())  # S=1
    if sc < state['score']:
       state.update(score=sc,a=a.copy(),c=c.copy(),S=1.0); print
    if max_seconds is not None:
       dl = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+max_seconds)
    else: dl=deadline
    if time.monotonic() < dl - 5:
       sc2, a2 = coordinate_descent(a, c, 1.0, len(a), dl, rng, ...)
       if sc2 < state['score'] - 1e-13:
          state.update(...)
    # If no improvement but initial better? already.
```

`conv_full(a)`:
```
n=len(a); M=2*n-1
L=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
out=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
return out[:M]
```
For real sequences. If n=0? no.

`resample_sequence`: If m=1? no. Use `np.interp` linear. Might introduce small negative if a has zeros? clip.

`normalize_sum(a, target=1.0)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy().ravel()
a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
s=float(a.sum())
if s <= 1e-12:
    return np.full(a.size if a.size else 64, target/a.size?) # if size 0? handle.
return a/s*target
```
If target=1. OK.

`score_of(a, c=None)`:
```
a=np.asarray...
if c is None: c=conv_full(a)
S=float(a.sum())
if S <= 1e-12: return inf
return 2*len(a)*float(np.max(c))/(S*S)
```

`top_indices(c,k)`:
```
L=len(c); k=min(k,L)
if k<=0: return np.array([],int)
idx=np.argpartition(c, L-k)[-k:] if k<L else np.arange(L)
idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
return idx
```
`np.argpartition(c, L-k)` with k=L? L-k=0 invalid? If k==L, return all sorted. Good.
`top_indices_forced(c,k,forced)`: Use top_indices and add forced. Need if forced duplicates. Use list.
```
def top_indices_forced(c,k,forced):
   idx=top_indices(c,k).tolist()
   L=len(c)
   for f in forced:
      if 0 <= f < L and f not in idx:
         idx.append(int(f))
   return np.array(idx, dtype=np.int64)
```
`f not in idx` O(k). fine.

Let's write candidates function:
```
def make_coordinates_candidates(a,c,S,n,j,x0,p_idx,max_a,avg):
    if max_a <= 1e-300: ma=avg
    else: ma=max_a
    if x0 > 1e-14:
       hi = x0 + 0.5*max(x0,avg)
    else:
       hi = max(10.0*ma, 50.0*avg)
    hi = max(hi, 10.0*ma, 50.0*avg)
    cap = max(5.0*hi, 200.0*avg, x0 + max(x0,avg))
    hi = min(hi, cap)
    if hi <= x0 + 1e-13:
       hi = x0 + max(10.0*avg, 1e-10)
    xg = surrogate_best_x(a,c,S,n,j,x0,p_idx,hi)
    step = max(0.25*max(x0,avg), 0.5*avg)
    cands=[xg, x0-step, x0+step]
    if x0 > 20.0*avg:
       cands.append(0.0)
    if x0 < 0.5*hi:
       cands.append(hi)
    # dedupe keep 4? We'll leave to caller with max_candidates
    return dedupe_candidates(cands, x0, hi)
```

Let's write `hamiltonian_sparse` efficiently:
```
def hamiltonian_sparse(a, lags, weights, n):
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx in range(lags.size):
        w = float(weights[idx])
        if w == 0.0: continue
        k = int(lags[idx])
        js = k - (n - 1)
        if js < 0: js = 0
        je = k
        if je > n - 1: je = n - 1
        if js > je: continue
        i_start = k - je
        i_end = k - js
        # a slice i_start:i_end+1 length je-js+1, reverse
        b[js:je+1] += w * a[i_start:i_end+1][::-1]
    return b
```
Check with n=3, k=4, js=2,je=2,i_start=2,i_end=2, a[2] to b2. Good.
Weights length same. If `weights` normalized but lags not sorted; okay.

KKT candidates: `b` can have zeros; mix. `cand = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * b`. Since both sum1? a sum1, b sum1. If b sum not exactly due to numerical, normalize b. Good. If mix=1, cand=b. Then `cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None); cs=cand.sum(); if cs>0 cand/=cs`. If b zeros in many coords, candidate sparse; score maybe bad. Mix small safe.

```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
   noise = rng.standard_normal(len(a)) * strength
   # smooth: if len>10, kernel 5
   if len(a)>10:
       kernel = np.ones(5)/5.0
       noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode='same')
   a2 = a * np.exp(noise)
   a2 = a2 + 1e-6
   return normalize_sum(a2,1.0)
```
Modulation similar.

def coordinate_descent(a_in, c_in, S_in, n_in, deadline, rng, p_size=128, max_exact_candidates=None):
    a = np.array(a_in, dtype=np.float64, copy=True).ravel()

    if n == 0:

    if n == 1:

       a = a/s
       c = np.array(c_in, dtype=np.float64, copy=True).ravel()

       else: c = c/(s*s)
       S = 1.0
    ...

def make_candidates(a,c,S,n,j,x0,p_idx,max_a,avg):
   if max_a <=0: max_a=avg
   if x0 > 1e-14:
       hi = x0 + 0.5*max(x0,avg)
   else:
       hi = 0.0
   hi = max(hi, 5.0*max_a, 20.0*avg)
   cap = max(20.0*max_a, 100.0*avg, x0 + max(x0,avg))
   if hi > cap: hi = cap
   if hi <= x0 + 1e-12:
       hi = x0 + max(10.0*avg, 1e-10)
   xg = surrogate_best_x(a,c,S,n,j,x0,p_idx,hi)
   cands=[xg]
   if x0 > 20.0*avg:
       cands.append(0.0)
   step = max(0.25*max(x0,avg), 0.5*avg)
   cands.append(x0 - step)
   cands.append(x0 + step)
   if x0 < 0.5*hi:
       cands.append(hi)
   return dedupe_candidates(cands, x0, hi)
```

`dedupe_candidates` top-level:
```
def dedupe_candidates(cands, x0, hi):
   out=[]
   tol=1e-12
   for val in cands:
      if not np.isfinite(val): continue
      x=float(val)
      if x < 0.0: x=0.0
      if hi is not None and x > hi: x=float(hi)
      duplicate=False
      for y in out:
         if abs(x-y) <= tol*max(1.0,abs(x),abs(y)):
             duplicate=True; break
      if not duplicate:
         out.append(x)
   return out
```
Good.

`run_candidate` top-level. It needs to update `state` dict. Use `state['score']` etc. Print.
```
def run_candidate(a_in, state, deadline, rng, label='candidate', max_seconds=None, p_size=None):
   a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size < 2: return
   a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s = float(a.sum())
   if s <= 1e-9:
      a = np.ones(a.size)/a.size
      s=1
   else: a = a/s
   c = conv_full(a)
   sc = 2.0*a.size*float(c.max())
   if sc + 1e-13 < state['score']:
      state['score']=sc; state['a']=a.copy(); state['c']=c.copy(); state['S']=1.0
      print(f"[{label}] initial {sc:.8f} n={a.size} t=...")
   else:
      print(f"[{label}] initial {sc:.8f} (best {state['score']:.8f})")
   now=time.monotonic()
   if max_seconds is not None:
      dl = min(deadline, now + max_seconds)
   else:
      dl = deadline
   if dl - now > 10:
      sc2, a2 = coordinate_descent(a, c, 1.0, a.size, dl, rng, p_size=p_size)
      if sc2 + 1e-13 < state['score']:
          state['score']=float(sc2); state['a']=np.asarray(a2).copy(); state['c']=conv_full(state['a']); state['S']=1.0
          print(...)
      else:

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total - 25.0
    state = make_state()
    state['start']=t_start
    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        parent_norm = normalize_sum(parent, 1.0)
        base_len = parent_norm.size
        ref = parent_norm
    else:
        base_len = 4096
        ref = random_smooth_initial(base_len, rng)
    print(f"base len {base_len} deadline ...")
    # main
    main_dl = t_start + 0.70 * (total - 25.0)
    run_candidate(ref, state, main_dl, rng, 'main')
    # if still no a? state has at least initial.
    # current best ref_if same length
    if state['a'] is not None and state['a'].size == base_len:
        ref = state['a']
    # Prepare alternates loop
    alt_labels = ['perturb-small','perturb-large','modulated','resample8192','lowres','random-same','random-alt']
    # We need candidate arrays; generate on fly.
    ntasks = len(alt_labels)
    for idx, label in enumerate(alt_labels):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline - 60: break
       remaining = deadline - now
       slots = ntasks - idx
       dl_task = min(deadline, now + max(45.0, remaining/(slots + 0.5)))
       cand = None
       if label == 'perturb-small':
          base = ref if ref.size==base_len else (parent if parent is not None else ref)
          cand = perturb_sequence(base, rng, 0.03)
       elif label == 'perturb-large': ...
       elif label == 'modulated': ...
       elif label == 'resample8192':
          target_len = 8192 if base_len < 8192 else min(10000, base_len)  # if base_len already 8192? maybe skip
          if target_len == base_len: continue? We can skip to avoid duplicate.
          cand = resample_sequence(base, target_len)
       elif label == 'lowres':
          base = ref if base else ...
          # low_res uses some time, then run_candidate with remaining part
          low_time = max(10.0, min(90.0, (dl_task-now)*0.4))
          cand = low_res_candidate(base_len, 256, base, rng, dl_task, low_time)
       elif label == 'random-same':
          cand = random_smooth_initial(base_len, rng)
       elif label == 'random-alt':
          target_len = 8192 if base_len < 8192 else base_len
          if target_len == base_len: cand = random_smooth_initial(base_len, rng)
          else: cand = random_smooth_initial(target_len, rng)
       if cand is not None:
          # For lowres, candidate already optimized lowdim; give it time
          run_candidate(cand, state, dl_task, rng, label)
    # final polish
    if state['a'] is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
       run_candidate(state['a'], state, deadline, rng, 'final')
    # output
    if state['a'] is None or state['score'] == np.inf:
       out_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
       out_arr = np.asarray(state['a'], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
       s = float(out_arr.sum())
       if s < 0.01:
          out_arr = out_arr / (s if s>0 else 1) maybe if s=0 ones
       # ensure finite
       out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    print(...)
    return out
```

`score_of`:
```
def score_of(a, c=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    if c is None:
        c = conv_full(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c) / (S * S))
```
If c from unnormalized a. Good.

`normalize_sum`:
```
def normalize_sum(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a[:] = 0.0
        a[0] = target
        return a
    return a * (target / s)
```
If a size 1 and target1, returns [1]. summary But coordinate n=1 returns score2; final okay. Maybe if a[0]=target, sum target.
But if target=1 and size0 -> a[0] okay.

`conv_full`: Need handle a maybe list.
```
def conv_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    M = 2*n - 1
    L = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return out[:M].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```

`load_parent`:
```
def load_parent():
    try:
       hs = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if hs is None: return None
       arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if arr.size < 2: return None
       arr = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
       if float(arr.sum()) <= 1e-9: return None
       return arr
    except Exception:
       return None
```
Good.

`top_indices`:
```
def top_indices(c, k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    k = int(k)
    if k <=0: return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    if k >= L:
       idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
       idx = np.argpartition(c, L - k, axis=0)[-k:].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
    idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx], kind='mergesort')[::-1]]
    return idx
```
`np.argpartition(..., axis=0)` okay 1D. If c contains NaN? no.

`top_indices_forced`:
```
def top_indices_forced(c,k,forced):
   L=len(c); idx=top_indices(c,k).tolist()
   out=[]
   seen=set()
   for i in idx:
      ii=int(i)
      if 0<=ii<L and ii not in seen:
          seen.add(ii); out.append(ii)
   for f in forced:
      fi=int(f)
      if 0<=fi<L and fi not in seen:
          seen.add(fi); out.append(fi)
   return np.array(out, dtype=np.int64)
```

`surrogate_value`:
```
def surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, x, x0, S):
    Sn = S + (x - x0)
    if Sn <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100
    denom = Sn * Sn
    val = base_out_max
    if base_lin.size > 0:
        dl = x - x0
        m = float(np.max(base_lin + 2.0 * dl * coeff))
        if m > val: val = m
    if base_quad_val > -1e300:
        q = base_quad_val + x * x
        if q > val: val = q
    return val / denom
```
If val is -inf and quad -inf? quad always. If val is inf due base_out? not. Good.

def surrogate_best_x(a,c,S,n,j,x0,lags,hi):
    if lags.size == 0:
       return x0
    if hi <= x0 + 1e-15:
       hi = x0 + max(S/n, 1e-10)
    seg = (lags >= j) & (lags < j + n)
    out = ~seg
    base = c[lags]
    if out.any(): base_out = float(np.max(base[out]))
    else: base_out = -np.inf
    lin = seg & (lags != 2*j)
    if lin.any():
        lin_lags = lags[lin]
        base_lin = base[lin]
        coeff = a[lin_lags - j]
    else:
        base_lin = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        coeff = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    base_quad = float(c[2*j]) - x0*x0
    lo=0.0; ub=float(hi)
    # evaluate f0? golden
    gr = (np.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    c1 = ub - gr*(ub-lo)
    c2 = lo + gr*(ub-lo)
    f1 = surrogate_value(...)
    f2 = surrogate_value(...)
    for _ in range(12):
       if f1 < f2:
          ub = c2; c2=c1; f2=f1; c1=ub - gr*(ub-lo); f1=surrogate_value(...)
       else:
          lo = c1; c1=c2; f1=f2; c2=lo + gr*(ub-lo); f2=surrogate_value(...)
    xg = 0.5*(lo+ub)
    # Compare with x0 and boundary? Maybe evaluate a few and choose min:
    best=xg; fb=surrogate_value(... xg)
    for xv in (0.0, ub, x0, c1, c2):
       fv=surrogate_value(...)
       if fv < fb: best=xv; fb=fv
    return float(best)
```

def kkt_refine(a, c, n, p_idx, deadline, max_seconds=30.0):
    best_sc = score_of(a,c) # or 2n max since c
    best_a = a.copy()
    if p_idx.size < 2: return best_sc,best_a
    end = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+max_seconds)
    m_vals=[]
    for m in (16, 64, 256):
       mm = min(m, p_idx.size)
       if mm>0 and mm not in m_vals: m_vals.append(mm)
    for m in m_vals:
       if time.monotonic() >= end: break
       lags = p_idx[:m]
       ct = c[lags]
       # weights list
       # uniform
       for wf in (0,1,2):
          if wf==0: w = np.full(m, 1.0/m)
          elif wf==1:
             ss=ct.sum();
             if ss<=0: continue
             w=ct/ss
          else:
             ww=ct*ct; ss=ww.sum(); if ss<=0: continue; w=ww/ss
          # maybe softmax only for m? add if m>4
          b=hamiltonian_sparse(a,lags,w,n)
          bs=b.sum()
          if bs <= 1e-15: continue
          b /= bs
          for mix in (0.1,0.3,0.6,0.9,1.0):
             if time.monotonic() >= end: break
             cand = (1.0-mix)*a + mix*b
             cand = np.clip(cand,0.0,None)
             cs=float(cand.sum())
             if cs <= 1e-12: continue
             cand /= cs
             sc = score_of(cand)
             if sc + 1e-12 < best_sc:
                 best_sc = float(sc); best_a = cand.copy()
    # Add softmax weights if enough time?
    if time.monotonic() < end and p_idx.size >= 16:
       lags = p_idx[:min(128,p_idx.size)] ...
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Maybe include softmax only for m=128 to reduce convs. Let's implement inside m loop if m==max m: include softmax. But `ct` small. Use temp.
```
       # if m == m_vals[-1], add softmax
```
Actually m_vals [16,64,256]. For each, 3 weights*5 mixes=15 conv; 3 m=45 conv. Plus softmax maybe 5 conv. okay. Could be 50 conv. Fine. 50*0.005=0.25s. Good.
But time max_seconds 30, not issue.

Let's write propose robustly:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total - 30.0
    state = make_state(); state['start']=t_start
    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
       base_len = int(parent.size)
       ref = normalize_sum(parent, 1.0)
       parent_ref = ref
    else:
       base_len = 4096
       ref = random_smooth_initial(base_len, rng)
       parent_ref = ref
    print(f"start len {base_len} time budget {total}")
    main_dl = t_start + 0.70 * (total - 30.0)
    run_candidate(ref, state, main_dl, rng, 'main')
    if state['a'] is not None and state['a'].size == base_len:
       base_ref = state['a']
    else:
       base_ref = parent_ref
    # tasks
    tasks=[]
    tasks.append(('perturb-small', 'perturb', 0.03))
    tasks.append(('perturb-large', 'perturb', 0.08))
    tasks.append(('modulated', 'mod', 0.0))
    tasks.append(('lowres', 'low', 0.0))
    tasks.append(('random-same', 'rand', 0.0))
    if base_len < 8192:
       tasks.append(('resample8192', 'resample', 8192))
       tasks.append(('random-8192', 'rand', 8192))
    for idx, task in enumerate(tasks):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline - 60: break
       remaining=deadline-now
       slots=len(tasks)-idx
       dl_task = min(deadline, now + max(60.0, remaining/(slots+0.5)))
       label, kind, arg = task
       cand=None; base_to_use = base_ref
       if kind == 'perturb':
          if base_to_use.size == 0: continue
          cand = perturb_sequence(base_to_use, rng, arg)
       elif kind == 'mod':
          cand = modulate_sequence(base_to_use, rng)
       elif kind == 'low':
          low_time = max(15.0, min(120.0, max(0.0, dl_task - now) * 0.4))
          cand = low_res_candidate(base_len, 256, base_to_use, rng, dl_task, low_time)
          dl_task = min(dl_task, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, (dl_task - time.monotonic())*0.8)) # not needed
       elif kind == 'rand':
          L = int(arg) if arg else base_len
          cand = random_smooth_initial(L, rng)
       elif kind == 'resample':
          L = int(arg)
          if L != base_to_use.size:
             cand = resample_sequence(base_to_use, L)
          else:
             cand = base_to_use.copy()
       if cand is not None:

```
def random_smooth_initial(n, rng):
    n=int(n)
    if n < 2: return np.ones(max(2,n))/...
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    a=np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(6):
       center=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.95)); width=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.25)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0))
       a += amp*np.exp(-((x-center)/width)**2)
    a += 0.05
    return normalize_sum(a,1.0)
```
Could use rng integers for number bumps. Fixed 6 okay.

`modulate_sequence`:
```
def modulate_sequence(a, rng):
   n=len(a)
   if n<2: return normalize_sum(a)
   x=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)/n
   a2=a.copy()
   for _ in range(4):
      freq = int(rng.integers(1,6))
      amp=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.05))
      phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      a2 *= (1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x + phase))
   a2 = np.clip(a2,0,None)
   return normalize_sum(a2,1.0)
```
Good.

Potential issue: State score for parent after main coordinate maybe from internal greedy with no_improve; final `score_of(state a)` maybe slightly lower/higher. `run_candidate` initial update uses score_of. If coordinate returns best_a, run_candidate computes `sc2 = score_of(state a?)`. Actually code:
```
sc2, a2 = coordinate_descent(...)
if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < state...:
    cand = normalize_sum(a2)
    scv = score_of(cand)
    if scv < state...: update
```
Good. If sc2 not finite but a2 maybe? Not.

Now, maybe if state score inf and coordinate returns inf? initial score set. Good.

Let's now write final code. Ensure no imported evaluate. Good.

One possible syntax issue: In `top_indices`, `idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx], kind='mergesort')[::-1]]` If idx empty? k>0. okay.

Let's produce final. We'll include comments but not too many. Final only code block. If any bugs, hopefully not.

Double-check variable names: In `coordinate_descent`, we use `S` variable but also `S_in`. Good.
In `make_candidates`, parameter `S` maybe not used except surrogate? surrogate_best_x uses S. Good.

Potential performance in `surrogate_best_x`: `base = c[lags]` copies. lags may include duplicates forced? top_indices_forced ensures no duplicates. Good.

Potential bug in `top_indices_forced`: It returns top k plus forced, but if k=0 returns empty forced. Good.

Now, final code below.
</think>

```python
"""
Step-function coefficient search with exact greedy coordinate descent,
active-peak surrogate line search, KKT-inspired sparse updates, reheating,
and multi-start candidates built from the provided parent and smooth resampling.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def conv_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return out[:m].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def normalize_sum(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a[:] = 0.0
        a[0] = float(target)
        return a
    return a * (float(target) / s)


def score_of(a, c=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    if c is None:
        c = conv_full(a)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (S * S))


def load_parent():
    try:
        hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if hs is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size < 2:
            return None
        arr = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(arr.sum()) <= 1e-9:
            return None
        return arr
    except Exception:
        return None


def top_indices(c, k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    if k >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, L - k)[-k:].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
    if idx.size:
        idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx], kind="mergesort")[::-1]]
    return idx


def top_indices_forced(c, k, forced):
    L = int(np.size(c))
    idx = top_indices(c, k).tolist()
    out = []
    seen = set()
    for i in idx:
        ii = int(i)
        if 0 <= ii < L and ii not in seen:
            seen.add(ii)
            out.append(ii)
    if forced is not None:
        for f in forced:
            fi = int(f)
            if 0 <= fi < L and fi not in seen:
                seen.add(fi)
                out.append(fi)
    return np.array(out, dtype=np.int64)


def apply_delta(a, c, n, j, delta, tmp):
    if abs(delta) <= 1e-300:
        return
    two = 2.0 * delta
    np.multiply(a, two, out=tmp)
    c[j:j + n] += tmp
    c[j + j] += delta * delta
    a[j] += delta


def surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, x, x0, S):
    Sn = S + (x - x0)
    if Sn <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100
    denom = Sn * Sn
    val = base_out_max
    if base_lin.size > 0:
        dl = x - x0
        m = float(np.max(base_lin + 2.0 * dl * coeff))
        if m > val:
            val = m
    if base_quad_val > -1e300:
        q = base_quad_val + x * x
        if q > val:
            val = q
    return val / denom


def surrogate_best_x(a, c, S, n, j, x0, lags, hi):
    if lags.size == 0:
        return x0
    if hi <= x0 + 1e-15:
        hi = x0 + max(S / float(n), 1e-10)

    seg = (lags >= j) & (lags < j + n)
    out = ~seg
    base = c[lags]

    if out.any():
        base_out_max = float(np.max(base[out]))
    else:
        base_out_max = -np.inf

    lin = seg & (lags != 2 * j)
    if lin.any():
        lin_lags = lags[lin]
        base_lin = base[lin]
        coeff = a[lin_lags - j]
    else:
        base_lin = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        coeff = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    # Lag 2j is always directly quadratic in x_j.
    base_quad_val = float(c[2 * j]) - x0 * x0

    lo = 0.0
    ub = float(hi)
    gr = (np.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0

    c1 = ub - gr * (ub - lo)
    c2 = lo + gr * (ub - lo)
    f1 = surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, c1, x0, S)
    f2 = surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, c2, x0, S)

    for _ in range(12):
        if f1 < f2:
            ub = c2
            c2 = c1
            f2 = f1
            c1 = ub - gr * (ub - lo)
            f1 = surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, c1, x0, S)
        else:
            lo = c1
            c1 = c2
            f1 = f2
            c2 = lo + gr * (ub - lo)
            f2 = surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, c2, x0, S)

    best = 0.5 * (lo + ub)
    best_f = surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, best, x0, S)
    for xv in (0.0, float(ub), float(x0), float(c1), float(c2)):
        fv = surrogate_value(base_out_max, base_lin, coeff, base_quad_val, xv, x0, S)
        if fv < best_f:
            best = float(xv)
            best_f = fv
    return float(best)


def dedupe_candidates(cands, x0, hi):
    out = []
    tol = 1e-12
    for val in cands:
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            continue
        x = float(val)
        if x < 0.0:
            x = 0.0
        if hi is not None and x > float(hi):
            x = float(hi)
        duplicate = False
        for y in out:
            if abs(x - y) <= tol * max(1.0, abs(x), abs(y)):
                duplicate = True
                break
        if not duplicate:
            out.append(x)
    return out


def make_candidates(a, c, S, n, j, x0, p_idx, max_a, avg):
    if max_a <= 1e-300:
        max_a = avg
    if x0 > 1e-14:
        hi = x0 + 0.5 * max(x0, avg)
    else:
        hi = 0.0
    hi = max(hi, 5.0 * max_a, 20.0 * avg)
    cap = max(20.0 * max_a, 100.0 * avg, x0 + max(x0, avg))
    if hi > cap:
        hi = cap
    if hi <= x0 + 1e-12:
        hi = x0 + max(10.0 * avg, 1e-10)

    xg = surrogate_best_x(a, c, S, n, j, x0, p_idx, hi)

    cands = [xg]
    if x0 > 20.0 * avg:
        cands.append(0.0)

    step = max(0.25 * max(x0, avg), 0.5 * avg)
    cands.append(x0 - step)
    cands.append(x0 + step)

    if x0 < 0.5 * hi:
        cands.append(hi)

    return dedupe_candidates(cands, x0, hi)


def hamiltonian_sparse(a, lags, weights, n):
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx in range(lags.size):
        w = float(weights[idx])
        if w == 0.0 or not np.isfinite(w):
            continue
        k = int(lags[idx])
        js = k - (n - 1)
        if js < 0:
            js = 0
        je = k
        if je > n - 1:
            je = n - 1
        if js > je:
            continue
        i_start = k - je
        i_end = k - js
        b[js:je + 1] += w * a[i_start:i_end + 1][::-1]
    return b


def kkt_refine(a, c, n, p_idx, deadline, max_seconds=30.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_sc = score_of(a, c)
    best_a = a.copy()
    if p_idx.size < 2 or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_sc, best_a

    end = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + float(max_seconds))
    m_vals = []
    for m in (16, 64, 256):
        mm = min(int(m), int(p_idx.size))
        if mm > 0 and mm not in m_vals:
            m_vals.append(mm)

    for m in m_vals:
        if time.monotonic() >= end:
            break
        lags = p_idx[:m]
        ct = c[lags]
        weights = []

        weights.append(np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64))

        ss = float(ct.sum())
        if ss > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ss):
            weights.append(ct.astype(np.float64) / ss)

        wt = ct * ct
        ss2 = float(wt.sum())
        if ss2 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ss2):
            weights.append(wt.astype(np.float64) / ss2)

        if m >= 16:
            temp = max(float(np.std(ct)), float(ct.max()) * 0.2, 1e-15)
            w = np.exp((ct - ct.max()) / temp)
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if sw > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sw):
                weights.append(w / sw)

        for w in weights:
            if time.monotonic() >= end:
                break
            w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
            if w.size != m:
                continue
            b = hamiltonian_sparse(a, lags, w, n)
            bs = float(b.sum())
            if bs <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(bs):
                continue
            b /= bs
            for mix in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.0):
                if time.monotonic() >= end:
                    break
                cand = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * b
                cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
                cs = float(cand.sum())
                if cs <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(cs):
                    continue
                cand /= cs
                sc = score_of(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc + 1e-12 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = cand.copy()
    return best_sc, best_a


def coordinate_descent(a_in, c_in, S_in, deadline, rng, p_size=128, max_exact_candidates=None):
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return 2.0, a

    old_s = float(a.sum())
    if old_s <= 1e-12:
        a[:] = 1.0 / n
        old_s = 1.0
    else:
        a = a / old_s

    c = np.asarray(c_in, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size != 2 * n - 1:
        c = conv_full(a)
    elif abs(old_s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        c = c / (old_s * old_s)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    if p_size is None:
        p_size = 128 if n >= 4000 else 64
    p_size = int(p_size)
    if max_exact_candidates is None:
        max_exact_candidates = 3 if n >= 5000 else 4
    max_exact_candidates = int(max_exact_candidates)

    two_n = 2.0 * n
    current_score = float(two_n * np.max(c))
    best_score = current_score
    best_a = a.copy()
    S = 1.0
    tmp = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    forced = (0, 2 * n - 2, n - 1)

    no_improve = 0
    pass_count = 0
    reheat_count = 0
    deadline = float(deadline)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 4:
        pass_count += 1
        pass_start = current_score
        p_idx = top_indices_forced(c, p_size, forced)
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        max_a = float(np.max(a))
        avg = S / float(n)

        if pass_count % 25 == 0:
            print(f"    coord pass {pass_count}: current={current_score:.9f} "
                  f"local_best={best_score:.9f} no_improve={no_improve} reheat={reheat_count}")

        accepted_in_pass = 0

        for count, jj in enumerate(order):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            if count > 0 and count % 256 == 0:
                p_idx = top_indices_forced(c, p_size, forced)
                max_a = float(np.max(a))
                avg = S / float(n)

            x0 = float(a[jj])
            cands = make_candidates(a, c, S, n, int(jj), x0, p_idx, max_a, avg)
            if len(cands) > max_exact_candidates:
                cands = cands[:max_exact_candidates]

            best_x = x0
            best_delta = 0.0
            best_sc = current_score

            for x in cands:
                delta = float(x) - x0
                if abs(delta) <= 1e-13 * max(1.0, abs(x0)):
                    continue
                S_new = S + delta
                if S_new <= 1e-12:
                    continue

                apply_delta(a, c, n, int(jj), delta, tmp)
                maxc = float(np.max(c))
                sc = float(two_n * maxc / (S_new * S_new))

                if sc + 1e-12 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_delta = delta
                    best_x = float(x)

                rollback = x0 - float(a[jj])
                if abs(rollback) > 0.0:
                    apply_delta(a, c, n, int(jj), rollback, tmp)

            if abs(best_delta) > 1e-15:
                apply_delta(a, c, n, int(jj), best_delta, tmp)
                S += best_delta
                current_score = best_sc
                accepted_in_pass += 1

                if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
                    best_score = current_score
                    if accepted_in_pass % 256 == 0 or (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 30.0:
                        best_a = a.copy()

                if S < 0.5 or S > 1.5:
                    oldS = S
                    a = a / oldS
                    c = c / (oldS * oldS)
                    S = 1.0
                    current_score = float(two_n * np.max(c))
                    if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
                        best_score = current_score

        # End of pass: renormalize and repair convolution by FFT.
        oldS = float(a.sum())
        if oldS <= 1e-12:
            break
        a = a / oldS
        c = conv_full(a)
        S = 1.0
        current_score = float(two_n * np.max(c))

        if current_score + 1e-9 < pass_start:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_a = a.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if (no_improve > 0 or pass_count % 12 == 0) and rem > 25.0:
            current_before = current_score
            p_big = top_indices(c, min(384, 2 * n - 1))
            sc_k, a_k = kkt_refine(a, c, n, p_big, deadline, max_seconds=min(30.0, rem))
            if np.isfinite(sc_k) and sc_k + 1e-11 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_k)
                best_a = np.asarray(a_k, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = conv_full(a)
                S = 1.0
                current_score = float(two_n * np.max(c))
                no_improve = 0
            elif np.isfinite(sc_k) and sc_k + 1e-11 < current_before:
                a = np.asarray(a_k, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
                a = normalize_sum(a, 1.0)
                c = conv_full(a)
                S = 1.0
                current_score = float(two_n * np.max(c))
                if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
                    best_score = current_score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0

        if no_improve >= 2 and reheat_count < 5 and rem > 120.0:
            strength = 0.015 + 0.018 * float(reheat_count)
            a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength)
            c = conv_full(a)
            S = 1.0
            current_score = float(two_n * np.max(c))
            reheat_count += 1
            no_improve = 0

    # Final exact evaluation of current state, while protected best is retained.
    cur_a = normalize_sum(a, 1.0)
    cur_c = conv_full(cur_a)
    cur_sc = float(two_n * np.max(cur_c))
    if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc + 1e-12 < best_score:
        best_score = cur_sc
        best_a = cur_a.copy()

    if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = normalize_sum(np.ones(n), 1.0)
    elif not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
        best_a = normalize_sum(np.ones(n), 1.0)

    return float(best_score), best_a


def random_smooth_initial(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        n = 2
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(6):
        center = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
        width = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.25))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
        a += amp * np.exp(-((x - center) / width) ** 2)
    a += 0.05
    return normalize_sum(a, 1.0)


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return normalize_sum(np.ones(64), 1.0)
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * float(strength)
    if n > 20:
        kernel = np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
    a2 = a * np.exp(noise)
    a2 = a2 + 1e-5 * max(1.0, float(strength))
    return normalize_sum(np.clip(a2, 0.0, None), 1.0)


def modulate_sequence(a, rng):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_sum(a, 1.0)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a2 = a.copy()
    for _ in range(4):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.05))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a2 *= (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))
    return normalize_sum(np.clip(a2, 0.0, None), 1.0)


def resample_sequence(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(m)
    n = a.size
    if m <= 0:
        m = n
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sum(np.clip(b, 0.0, None), 1.0)


def low_res_candidate(base_n, m, base_a, rng, deadline, low_seconds):
    now = time.monotonic()
    low_dl = min(float(deadline), now + float(low_seconds))
    if base_a is not None and base_a.size > 0:
        a = resample_sequence(base_a, m)
    else:
        a = random_smooth_initial(m, rng)
    a = normalize_sum(a, 1.0)
    c = conv_full(a)
    _, a2 = coordinate_descent(a, c, 1.0, low_dl, rng, p_size=48, max_exact_candidates=4)
    return resample_sequence(a2, base_n)


def make_state():
    return {
        "score": np.inf,
        "a": None,
        "c": None,
        "S": 1.0,
        "start": time.monotonic(),
    }


def run_candidate(a_in, state, deadline, rng, label="candidate", p_size=None):
    a = np.asarray(a_in, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size < 2:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-9:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    else:
        a = a / s

    c = conv_full(a)
    sc = float(2.0 * a.size * np.max(c))
    t0 = float(state.get("start", time.monotonic()))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = np.inf

    if sc + 1e-13 < float(state["score"]):
        state["score"] = sc
        state["a"] = a.copy()
        state["c"] = c.copy()
        state["S"] = 1.0
        print(f"[{label}] initial {sc:.9f}, n={a.size}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")
    else:
        print(f"[{label}] initial {sc:.9f}, best={float(state['score']):.9f}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < float(deadline) - 10.0:
        sc2, a2 = coordinate_descent(a, c, 1.0, float(deadline), rng, p_size=p_size)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 + 1e-13 < float(state["score"]):
            cand = normalize_sum(a2, 1.0)
            scv = score_of(cand)
            if np.isfinite(scv) and scv + 1e-13 < float(state["score"]):
                state["score"] = float(scv)
                state["a"] = cand
                state["c"] = conv_full(cand)
                state["S"] = 1.0
                print(f"[{label}] improved to {float(state['score']):.9f}, n={cand.size}, t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")
            else:
                print(f"[{label}] coordinate {float(sc2):.9f}, verified {float(scv):.9f}")
        else:
            print(f"[{label}] coordinate {float(sc2):.9f}")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + budget - 25.0

    state = make_state()
    state["start"] = t_start

    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        base_len = int(parent.size)
        ref = normalize_sum(parent, 1.0)
        parent_ref = ref
    else:
        base_len = 4096
        ref = random_smooth_initial(base_len, rng)
        parent_ref = ref

    print(f"parent len={base_len}, budget={budget:.0f}s, deadline offset={budget-25.0:.0f}s")

    # Main local search from the best known/parent-like state.
    main_dl = t_start + 0.70 * (budget - 25.0)
    run_candidate(ref, state, main_dl, rng, "main", p_size=None)

    if state["a"] is not None and int(state["a"].size) == base_len:
        base_ref = np.asarray(state["a"], dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    else:
        base_ref = parent_ref

    tasks = [
        ("perturb-small", "perturb", 0.03),
        ("perturb-large", "perturb", 0.07),
        ("modulated", "mod", 0.0),
        ("lowres-256", "low", 0.0),
        ("random-same", "rand", base_len),
    ]
    if base_len < 8192:
        tasks.append(("resample8192", "resample", 8192))
        tasks.append(("random-8192", "rand", 8192))

    for idx, task in enumerate(tasks):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 60.0:
            break
        label, kind, arg = task
        remaining = float(deadline) - now
        slots = len(tasks) - idx
        task_dl = min(float(deadline), now + max(60.0, remaining / (slots + 0.5)))

        cand = None
        if kind == "perturb":
            if base_ref.size > 0:
                cand = perturb_sequence(base_ref, rng, float(arg))
        elif kind == "mod":
            if base_ref.size > 0:
                cand = modulate_sequence(base_ref, rng)
        elif kind == "low":
            low_time = max(15.0, min(120.0, max(0.0, task_dl - now) * 0.4))
            if base_ref.size > 0:
                cand = low_res_candidate(base_len, 256, base_ref, rng, task_dl, low_time)
        elif kind == "rand":
            L = int(arg)
            if L > 0:
                cand = random_smooth_initial(L, rng)
        elif kind == "resample":
            L = int(arg)
            if L > 0 and base_ref.size > 0 and L != base_ref.size:
                cand = resample_sequence(base_ref, L)
            elif base_ref.size > 0:
                cand = base_ref.copy()

        if cand is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
            run_candidate(cand, state, task_dl, rng, label, p_size=None)

    # Final polishing of the best object if any time remains.
    if state["a"] is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        run_candidate(state["a"], state, deadline, rng, "final-polish", p_size=None)

    if state["a"] is None or not np.isfinite(state["score"]):
        out_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = np.asarray(state["a"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        out_arr = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)

    # Keep scale reasonable for the evaluator; objective is scale invariant.
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out_arr = normalize_sum(out_arr, 1.0)

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    if (not np.isfinite(verified)) or verified > 1e6:
        out_list = [0.5] * 1024
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
        except Exception:
            verified = float("inf")

    print(f"FINAL internal={float(state['score']):.9f}, verified={verified:.9f}, len={len(out_list)}, time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s")
    return out_list
```
